None => Some(ValidContentType::ApplicationJson),\n135.         };\n136. \n137.         // errors.has_errors() must be checked first, because it's possible for\n138.         // content_type to be Some, but other errors to have occurred.\n139.         if errors.has_errors() {\n140. (x.major, x.minor, x.patch)\n180. }\n181. \n182. fn semver_expr(\n183.     dropshot: &TokenStream,\n184.     span: proc_macro2::Span,\n185.     x: &VersionSpecifier,\n186. ) -> TokenStream {\n187.     match x {\n188. ApiEndpointKind::Regular(endpoint_fn) => endpoint_fn.span(),\n217.             ApiEndpointKind::Stub { attr, .. } => {\n218.                 // We need to point at the closest possible span to the actual\n219.                 // error, but we can't point at something nice like the\n220.                 // function name. That's because if we do, rust-analyzer will\n221.                 // produce a lot of irrelevant results when ctrl-clicking on\n222.                 // the function name.\n223.                 //\n224.                 // So we point at the `#`, which seems out-of-the-way enough\n225.                 // for successful generation while being close by for errors.\n226.                 // Seems pretty unobjectionable.\n227.                 attr.pound_token.span()\n228.             }\n229.         };\n230. \n231.         // Set the span for all of the bits and pieces. VersionRange::All => {\n264.                 quote_spanned! {span=> #dropshot::ApiEndpointVersions::All }\n265.             }\n266.             VersionRange::From(x) => {\n267.                 let v = semver_expr(dropshot, span, &x);\n268.                 quote_spanned! {span=>\n269.                     #dropshot::ApiEndpointVersions::From(\n270.                         #v\n271.                     )\n272.                 }\n273.             }\n274.             VersionRange::Until(y) => {\n275.                 let v = semver_expr(dropshot, span, &y);\n276.                 quote_spanned! {span=>\n277.                     #dropshot::ApiEndpointVersions::Until(\n278.                         #v\n279.                     )\n280.                 }\n281.             }\n282.             VersionRange::FromUntil(x, y) => {\n283.                 let x = semver_expr(dropshot, span, &x);\n284.                 let y = semver_expr(dropshot, span, &y);\n285.                 quote_spanned! {span=>\n286.                     #dropshot::ApiEndpointVersions::from_until(\n287.                         #x,\n288.                         #y,\n289.                     ).unwrap()\n290.                 }\n291.             }\n292.         };\n293. \n294.         let fn_call = match kind {\n295.             ApiEndpointKind::Regular(endpoint_fn) => {\n296.                 quote_spanned! {span=>\n297.                     #dropshot::ApiEndpoint::new(\n298.                         #operation_id.to_string(),\n299.                         #endpoint_fn,\n300.                         #dropshot::Method::#method_ident,\n301.                         #content_type,\n302.                         #path,\n303.                         #versions,\n304.                     )\n305.                 }\n306.             }\n307.             ApiEndpointKind::Stub { attr: _, extractor_types, ret_ty } => {\n308.                 quote_spanned! {span=>\n309.                     #dropshot::ApiEndpoint::new_for_types::<(#(#extractor_types,)*), #ret_ty>(\n310.                         #operation_id.to_string(),\n311.                         #dropshot::Method::#method_ident,\n312.                         #content_type,\n313.                         #path,\n314.                         #versions,\n315.                     )\n316.                 }\n317.             }\n318.         };\n319. \n320.         quote_spanned! {span=>\n321.             #fn_call\n322.             #summary\n323.             #description\n324.             #(#tags)*\n325.             #visible\n326.             #deprecated\n327.             #request_body_max_bytes\n328.         }\n329.     }\n330. }\n331. \n332. #[derive(Debug)]\n333. pub(crate) enum VersionRange {\n334.     All,\n335.     From(VersionSpecifier),\n336.     Until(VersionSpecifier),\n337.     FromUntil(VersionSpecifier, VersionSpecifier),\n338. }\n339. \n340. impl syn::parse::Parse for VersionRange {\n341.     fn parse(input: syn::parse::ParseStream) -> syn::Result<Self> {\n342.         let lookahead = input.lookahead1();\n343.         if lookahead.peek(syn::Token![..]) {\n344.             let _ = input.parse::<syn::Token![..]>()?;\n345.             if input.is_empty() {\n346. String::from(\n416.                         \"semver pre-release string is not supported here\",\n417.                     ),\n418.                 ))\n419.             }\n420.         })\n421.         .and_then(|s| {\n422.             if s.build == semver::BuildMetadata::EMPTY {\n423.                 Ok(s)\n424.             } else {\n425.                 Err(syn::Error::new_spanned(\n426.                     v,\n427.                     String::from(\"semver build metadata is not supported here\"),\n428.                 ))\n429.             }\n430.         })\n431. }\n432. \n433. #[allow(non_snake_case)]\n434. #[derive(Deserialize, Debug)]\n435. pub(crate) enum ChannelProtocol {\n436.     WEBSOCKETS,\n437. }\n438. \n439. #[derive(Deserialize, Debug)]\n440. pub(crate) struct ChannelMetadata {\n441.     pub(crate) protocol: ChannelProtocol,\n442.     #[serde(default)]\n443.     pub(crate) operation_id: Option<String>,\n444.     pub(crate) path: String,\n445.     #[serde(default)]\n446.     pub(crate) tags: Vec<String>,\n447.     #[serde(default)]\n448.     pub(crate) unpublished: bool,\n449.     #[serde(default)]\n450.     pub(crate) deprecated: bool,\n451.     pub(crate) _dropshot_crate: Option<String>,\n452.     pub(crate) versions: Option<ParseWrapper<VersionRange>>,\n453. }\n454. \n455. impl ChannelMetadata {\n456.     /// Returns the dropshot crate value as a TokenStream.\n457.     pub(crate) fn dropshot_crate(&self) -> TokenStream {\n458.         get_crate(self._dropshot_crate.as_deref())\n459.     }\n460. \n461.     /// Validates metadata, returning a `ValidatedChannelMetadata` is valid.\n462.     ///\n463.     /// Note: the only reason we pass in attr here is to provide a span for\n464.     /// error reporting.
